When it matters, carry amount, sign, denomination, unit and state as printed, retaining how each value was derived. Record action status only where evidenced. A speaker’s intention, chart on a page and later resulting balance have different authority. Give directed changes their own rows if reciprocity or disagreement matters. Keep comparisons across distinct episodes and significant form choices directly retrievable.

## Claim and forecast history

A row marked "independent prospect" was frozen in the specialist's own lane. In V01–V05 the specialists saw each volume's front cover and flap before their first freeze, and the reviewer saw them on its first contact sheet; the principals did not see them until after MAIN_EXIT. The [checkpoint errata](checkpoints/FX_KURUMI_CHECKPOINT_ERRATA.md) give every such row's exposure disposition for V01–V04, and the [V05 checkpoint](checkpoints/FX_KURUMI_V05_CHECKPOINT.md#7-exposure-and-contamination-record) for V05. A row whose predictive credit changes records the credit separately from the observed outcome (2026-11-22).

| FXK-V05-C001 | V05's governing thesis: threads of money, concealed information and learned habit bind the four; Mochiko's creed (concealment, dominance, the fair arena, loss-cutting) is given a biography of neglect and humiliation, bound to it by repeated lines ("施し", friends; E061), and meets three tests — Yasuko's claim to reciprocity, the leak of her position through Yasuko to Kurumi, the exposure of her lie in Mebuki's hearing; Mebuki escalates "rescue" shorts the narrator calls "ただのポジション", admits error, cannot realize the loss, resigns; Kurumi brackets a greed-framed long, is paid at 120.05 while away from the screen, finds herself by her own after-tax reckoning still "あと300万円" short, is tempted by "億の世界" and checked by the memory of her father's slap, then opens a 300-lot short sized to the gap, with a take-profit and no stop shown; responsibility refined (Yasuko concedes 自己責任 and claims friendship) (principal, EVALUATION) | Principal's running notes; MAIN_EXIT | i0006–i0053, i0060–i0102, i0129–i0146, i0151–i0162; narrator i0090, i0095; closing image i0162. Against: §D.2 | NEW; EVAL on OBS HIGH and form; revised at reconciliation (reviewer C-01, C-07); V05 reading §D.1 |
| FXK-V05-C002 | Competing thesis: V05 is chiefly a sympathetic origin story; Mochiko's coldness is self-protection; the loan a disclosed hedge Mebuki chose and Yasuko accepts as 自己責任; the café injury accidental; the real danger is Kurumi's grief and restitution drive, which turns a nearly reached target into a larger bet (principal; wording revised at reconciliation, C-19) | Principal's running notes | For: i0018–i0039, i0110–i0126, i0133, i0139. Against: i0048, i0130–i0131, i0138, i0144; her doctrine exempts her own family support (i0028) | NEW; OPEN (live); §D.2, §L |

For prospects preserve predictions, confidence, disconfirming signals and actual exposure before resolution. Attribute independent tests separately. For revisions use `PRESERVE`, `STRENGTHEN`, `REVISE`, `DOWNGRADE`, `REJECT` or `OPEN`. Keep the historical argument and use a new linked entry for a material change.

## Person-level qualification

Subjects assessed through V07 (FXK-JP-V01-W01, FXK-JP-V02-W01, FXK-JP-V03-W01, FXK-JP-V04-W01, FXK-JP-V05-W01, FXK-JP-V06-W01 and FXK-JP-V07-W01) are listed after this paragraph. Record each eligible person, source and horizon, distinct habits, ordinary choices, Japanese speech, cognition under differing conditions, directional ties, counterexamples, evidence sufficiency and reassessment threshold. Link a completed monograph and its separate self-containment and fidelity decisions once accepted.

- **福賀くるみ (Fukuga Kurumi)**: source W01 V01; horizon ages 15 (2008–09) and 20 (Feb–May 2014). Evidence
  sufficiency: HIGH; she is on nearly every main page.
  - Distinct habits: compulsive chart-checking (commute, work, walk home); war and shōnen self-framing; frugality
    (umbrella); studying; cheerful performance to her father.
  - Ordinary choices: a commuting university for her father's sake; 特待生; part-time convenience-store work; WcDon
    lunches; a small drink in the student lounge; cardigans and ribbon ties; the "Live & Let Die" mug; Mochiko saved in
    her phone as hiragana "もちこ".
  - Speech: first person 私; casual じゃん/もん/よね; service register at work (いらっしゃいませー); masculine-leaning
    self-commands under strain (いくぞ, 行けっ, 上がれ); coarse under anger (なってンだっけ, ふざけんな); childlike under
    panic (やだやだ, やめてよぉ); polite emphasis (いりません).
  - Cognition under differing conditions: strategic and risk-aware when calm (i0042–i0045, i0064–i0066); euphoric
    after gains (i0049, i0073); rationalizing under moderate loss (i0089–i0097); sober flashes mid-crisis ("今はまだ
    耐えただけ…", i0122); felt incapacity under loss of family money (i0131, i0135); globally self-condemning at the
    nadir (i0139, i0141).
  - Directional ties: mother (grief, guilt, identification); father (love, concealment, unconsented use of his
    savings); Mochiko (trust, care for her as a lender, one lie, gratitude); Yūko (friend, offstage).
  - Counterexamples: refusals of all-in, 100枚 and the loan, partly out of worry for the lender ("困るのは萌智子でしょ?",
    i0066); a fast exit in Trade 1; a five-year deferral to legal age.
  - Reassessment threshold: any V02 scene of restitution or disclosure to the father; her interpretation of the win;
    whether she adopts stop orders or sizes up.
  - **V02 update** (FXK-JP-V02-W01; horizon late May – 15 July 2014, age 20). Evidence sufficiency: HIGH.
    - Distinct habits: still works the convenience-store job (i0012); reads savings books at night (i0084); a
      bookshop habit and a book-buying binge topped by "占い師のなり方" as a coping plan (i0094–i0096); keeps her
      first-grade "くるみのへや" door sign (i0114); watches live charts while "retired" (i0092, i0116); minimal assent
      under pressure ("…うん", "う…ん", "え…うん"; i0038, i0055, i0087).
    - Ordinary choices: a tote bag lettered "Y・H・V・H"; a hairclip; a high school spent only studying (i0093); a
      childhood drawing habit and the dream of being a kindergarten teacher (i0111–i0112); pachinko tried once,
      disliked, and regretted as books not bought (i0098–i0099).
    - Speech: 私; childish wheedling to her father while confessing (i0029); flustered refusals; a menacing teacher
      mode (i0065); soft, unrepeated requests (i0115); "戦う" for trading (i0017, i0131, i0140); address 萌智子,
      芽吹ちゃん, やす子ちゃん.
    - Cognition under differing conditions: outcome justification when cornered (i0029); luck attribution under her
      father's plea and in private (i0033, i0041, i0087–i0088, i0101); craving at others' gains (i0072, i0086);
      minimizing at thresholds ("様子見程度", "ちょろっと", "大人の遊び"; i0092, i0097, i0099); self-licensing after
      one open gain (i0129–i0131); a contrarian rule drawn from Mebuki's misses (i0141); a mild public account of the
      relapse (i0140) after a private vow of self-destruction (i0132).
    - Directional ties: father (concealment broken; assent without commitment; his worry acknowledged and
      overridden, i0130); Mochiko (doubt, then a bantering alliance of mutual use, i0145); Mebuki (reluctant adviser,
      then silent user of her views, i0141); Yasuko (kind host, i0150–i0152); mother (memory, i0111–i0112).
    - Counterexamples: structural de-risking (i0017); intent to file tax (i0018); several resisted impulses (i0092,
      i0096, i0099, i0116); an attempt to end the advising (i0115); "誰か私を止めて…!" (i0109).
    - Money: renewed trading with no pair, size, entry or stop shown; domestic balance 2,100,000 (deposit undated)
      → 2,656,690 (i0102, i0158).
    - Reassessment threshold: the first trade shown with pair and size; any loss; any disclosure to the father or to
      Mebuki; whether the narrator's "呪い" pays off.
  - **V03 update** (FXK-JP-V03-W01; horizon late July – late October 2014, age 20). Evidence sufficiency: HIGH overall;
    MODERATE for V03 alone (about 25 pages; she is offstage for most of Mebuki's story).
    - Distinct habits: trades during class on her phone (i0055); shares positions with Mochiko by phone and in lectures
      (i0010–i0011, i0053–i0055); draws mascot doodles; hand-makes rule sheets with hearts (i0086); announces orders as
      "○○枚発射" (i0138).
    - Ordinary choices: lives with her father, so her home is off-limits for the group (i0080); a part-time job, seen at a
      convenience-store register (i0026, i0154); TOEIC summer course and classes (i0052, i0124); pays her own share (i0124);
      does not know コミチア (i0082).
    - Speech: 私; cheerful casual with hearts; playful politeness to the group; minimizing ("軽く30枚くらい", "レバ20倍は低レバ");
      macro jargon (地政学リスク, QE3, ドル安調整; i0122); address 萌智子, 芽吹ちゃん, やす子ちゃん.
    - Cognition under differing conditions: forgone gain → "次はもっと欲張ろう" and a 2500万 target (i0053–i0054); open gain →
      regret at not sizing up (i0056); large win → "今回は欲張っちゃった" (i0091) then a self-limiting 1,000,000 withdrawal
      "無茶して失敗しないように" (i0124); reasoned bullish case (i0121–i0122).
    - Directional ties: Mochiko (open trust; she knew of Mochiko's earlier gains yet believes the false −10%, i0090;
      unaware of Mochiko's lies, of her contempt and of her disclosure of how Kurumi won in V01, framed as "借金");
      Mebuki (asks Mochiko whether Mebuki has been in touch, i0057; concern about school, i0103 by chat icon, i0120; her July
      trade kept quiet under the contest frame and a stay-out answer on the pound without a reason, i0024–i0026; never
      learns of Mebuki's position or plight); Yasuko (curious, inclusive, i0082–i0085); father (mentioned only, i0080).
    - Counterexamples: the withdrawal and tax reserve (i0124); no overseas account shown (labelled boxes 国内口座).
    - Money: EUR/USD short 40枚 ("43万円" by Mebuki's report); USD/JPY 30枚 at 104.221 realized ≈+1,562,700 (exit ≈109.43,
      CALC); 4,611,715 (73.5%増) → withdrawal → 3,611,715; USD/JPY 60枚 at 106.789, +456,600 open (i0154). Every trade she closes is profitable; no loss, no stop.
    - Reassessment threshold: her first depicted loss and whether she holds it (V01 C010 still untested); learning of Mebuki's
      plight or of Mochiko's lies; any present-time scene with her father.
  - **V04 update** (FXK-JP-V04-W01; horizon 31 October – 5 November 2014, age 20). Evidence sufficiency: HIGH overall; MODERATE
    for V04 alone (about 30 pages; offstage after i0115).
    - Distinct habits: announces orders as battle commands ("全力 買い(ロング)…!", "120枚発射…!!", i0006); laughs behind her door at a
      large open gain (i0007–i0008); models risk in diagrams to herself (i0113); conceals her open profit playfully ("秘密…っ♥",
      i0049).
    - Ordinary choices: shops for and tries on a recruit suit ("就活嫌だなぁ…", "もう少し学生したいよ", i0035, i0065); drawn to full-time
      trading, she retracts: "あ…嘘だよ! 世間体だってあるし! FXは勝ち逃げする予定だし!" (i0036); cooks curry for her father and eats alone
      when he is late (i0115); a two-storey apartment block with an external walkway (i0065); her room with bookshelves, PC desk,
      full-length mirror and a framed family photograph (i0065, i0072); her contact with Mebuki has thinned (i0051).
    - Speech: 私; hearts and "えへ♡"; gentle address to a junior ("あのね 芽吹ちゃん", i0068); firmness without leaving the casual
      register ("うん それは 穏やかじゃないね?", i0067; "だめ… 貸さない…", i0071); desu/masu in a chat report ("共有です", "注意です",
      i0076); hedged self-assessment ("冷静な判断… だと思う…", i0115).
    - Cognition under differing conditions: open gain → an all-in add at a new extreme for "税引き後2000万円" (i0005–i0006); larger gain →
      unease ("不気味", "要警戒") and a refusal to add "根拠がなさすぎる" (i0112–i0115); a friend's crisis → care with a limit (food money),
      refusal on protective grounds (i0067–i0072), then, alone, her father's maxim (i0074) and self-doubt ("最初からFXを教えた…?",
      i0075); accused of hypocrisy → "運がよかっただけ…で…" (i0073).
    - Directional ties: Mebuki (concern grounded in her mother, i0053–i0054; refusal and advice to withdraw, i0068–i0072; no further
      contact); Yasuko (the trusted channel for the warning, i0076); Mochiko (playful concealment; she discloses her mother to her, i0054;
      unaware of Mochiko's lies, loan and private label "狂人(くるみ)"); father (his V02 line adopted, i0074; "最近遅いな…", i0115).
    - Counterexamples: the declined second add (i0115) against the first (i0006); "勝ち逃げ" said (i0036), nothing realized.
    - Money: 180枚 USD/JPY long at 110.271 (120枚 added at 112.012); +7,812,000 open at 114.611 (CALC exact); balance 3,611,715
      unchanged; no stop; CALC ≈29.9× at the add, a tension with a 25× maximum printed only for Yasuko's account.
    - Disclosure: tells Mochiko and Yasuko that her mother killed herself because of FX (i0054) — first in the corpus; the causal link is
      her own.
    - Reassessment threshold: her first loss or her first realization of the 180枚; learning of Mochiko's loan or lies; her university
      year; any scene with her father.
  - **V05 update** (FXK-JP-V05-W01; horizon April 2012 (flashback) and 17 November – a night on or after 2 December 2014; 21 by the accepted V01 captions).
    Evidence sufficiency: HIGH (about 35 pages; decisive acts, thought and figures).
    - Distinct habits: battle commands for orders ("300枚発射…!", i0158); scenario diagrams to herself (Patterns A/B/C, i0078–i0079);
      spinning in her chair while deciding (i0079); sings while drying her hair (i0149); talks to her mother's photograph (i0159).
    - Ordinary choices: in the 2012 新都大学 経済学部 intake (orientation, i0042–i0044); the "Y・H・V・H" tote (i0044); dries dishes
      in an apron for her father (i0069); soaks dishes before her bath (i0147); strawberry-print pyjamas; the "くるみのへや" door sign
      (i0070, i0149); quits her part-time job citing 就活 (i0083); pachinko once, "微妙" (i0083, consistent with V02); a duffle coat
      and university-café coffee (i0143).
    - Speech: 私; "私 福賀くるみ!", "一緒に頑張ろうね!" (2012); "子供じゃないんだよ〜?", "私のことは気にせず行ってきて♪" to her father;
      polite to a co-worker ("就活って言ったじゃないですか", "すいません…"); "日和ってなんかいられない…!", "はした金"; "嘘はよくないよ!"
      to Mochiko; "やす子ちゃん"; "ママ" as a child (memory), "お母さん" now.
    - Cognition under differing conditions: a large open gain → regret of past prudence (i0070), a near-close stopped by her mother's
      face, "全然足りてない", greed embraced, then a protective bracket (i0072–i0080); the realized win → euphoria, then luck, then
      grief and counterfactual ("お母さんだってやる時期がよかったら…", i0153), the "億の世界" temptation checked by the memory of her father's slap (i0154), a
      retirement vow conditional on the target and emptiness ("灰色の人生", i0155), then a top call and a 300-lot short at her V01 size
      sized to the remaining target (i0157–i0158); "これで私の戦いは
      おしまい" with the PC off (i0159).
    - Directional ties: father (supportive of his transfer; he hesitates, i0069; his remembered slap a brake, i0154); mother (photograph, memory, "私やったよ",
      i0154–i0161); Mochiko (warm since 2012; calls out her lie, i0144); Yasuko (her information source, i0144); Mebuki (no exchange;
      Mebuki hides from her, i0143).
    - Counterexamples: the bracket and the self-check "ダメダメ! …FX引退しなきゃ!" (i0155) against the greed creed and the reversal.
    - Money: 180枚 long closed at 120.05, +17,602,200, balance 21,213,915 (i0152; CALC exact); new 300枚 short at 119.631, limit
      118.6 (+3,093,000 if filled, CALC), no stop shown (i0158); "あと300万円だけ" after tax (i0153): the target not yet met;
      the gap reconciles with accepted V03 figures (INF MOD-HIGH).
    - Disclosure: none new to others; she learns from Yasuko that Mochiko is long (i0144).
    - Year: the 2012 intake makes her a second-year at V01's 14 February 2014 caption and a third-year from April 2014 (V04-U03
      resolved at reconciliation).
    - Reassessment threshold: the 300-lot short's first figure and any stop; retirement or not; learning of the loan; the father's
      transfer and his lateness.
  - **V06 update** (FXK-JP-V06-W01; horizon 1998 and May 2006 – October 2008 (flashback, as a child of about 5 and 12–15) and 4–25
    December 2014; 21 by the accepted V01 captions). Evidence sufficiency: HIGH (most present-time pages; decisive acts, thought and
    figures).
    - Distinct habits: scenario diagrams to herself (Patterns A–D, i0008–i0011); battle words for orders ("発射——", i0117); fantasy staging
      of decisions (storm, bridge, knights; i0012, i0121–i0137); talks to her mother (i0090); swats away her own cautious child-self
      (i0094); a finger-tapping tic while the rate goes against her ("トントン", i0007).
    - Ordinary choices: hoodie and strawberry-print pyjamas (i0007, i0032); the "くるみのへや" door sign (i0007); a framed family photo
      (i0107, INF MOD); a handmade rule sheet with rabbit doodles kept since summer (i0143); beer and yakiniku at Christmas, a charred
      kalbi (i0142–i0154). As a child: pokes the 1998 PC (i0058), washes dishes, cooks "プリン雑炊" ("まっず!") and a "普通の雑炊" (i0073,
      i0085).
    - Speech: 私 (one あたし in banter by balloon placement, i0153); "お母さん"/"ママ" (1998), "お父さん"; "芽吹ちゃん", "やす子ちゃん",
      "萌智子"; "ほら… 下がったじゃん…", "やっぱ損切りいらなかったじゃん", "撤退できるわけがない", "あえてしてない!"; as a 2006
      middle-schooler "なんかダサい!", "車が気をつけるべき!".
    - Cognition under differing conditions: an open loss → refusal of a stop, then a norm-based cut (i0009–i0013); a realized loss →
      normalization, then hindsight (i0014–i0015); a second loss → regret of the V05 profit-take, "損小利大", arena-shifting
      (i0046–i0049); her mother's journal → "次は絶対勝つ" (i0090); uncertainty → certainty-seeking ("必勝の通貨", i0103); a named danger →
      reassurance and an all-out long without a stop order (i0128–i0137, i0152); a self-admitted 128円 ruin scenario held without a stop
      (i0010–i0011).
    - Directional ties: mother (grief, identification, restitution, i0090–i0095); Mochiko (affectionate accountability, i0143; open about
      her position, i0148); Yasuko (teasing, i0146); Mebuki ("残念…", credit for the contest, i0100–i0101; knows she quit FX and has a job, i0140, source unshown; nothing of the wipe-out);
      father (mentioned, i0093).
    - Counterexamples: two realized losses (i0013, i0046); refusal of a feel trade on EUR/USD for lack of a guarantee (i0106); a pause on
      a bad feeling (i0122); a discretionary switch-out in her first bridge plan (i0123–i0124); a first cut vindicated the same night
      (i0044–i0046).
    - Money: −1,743,000 and −1,131,000 realized; 18,339,915 (i0046, i0095); target "あと566万…とんで85円" (i0096); EUR/CHF 300枚 long,
      no stop, entry unprinted (i0148, i0152); "1000は超えてる" (i0147).
    - Reassessment threshold: the EUR/CHF fate and entry; the contest result; learning of Mebuki's wipe-out; her father's transfer.
  - **V07 update** (FXK-JP-V07-W01; horizon 25 December 2014 – 16/18 January 2015; 21 by the accepted V01 captions). Evidence sufficiency:
    HIGH (on most main pages; decisive figures, thought and speech).
    - Distinct habits: greets her mother's photograph on leaving ("じゃあお母さん 行ってきます", i0073); an octopus speaker for FX alerts
      (i0055); sing-song self-talk and puns on Mochiko's name (i0037, i0101); brushes her teeth reading market news (i0071); closes what she
      cannot face (the trading window, i0106; the photograph turned face down, i0113; mail deleted around the demand, i0127).
    - Ordinary choices: lives alone from the New Year (i0031); plain room, unread "自分探し" hobby books, among them a VTuber primer (i0049); experimental cooking ("プリン
      雑炊", mochi with an energy drink, i0048–i0049) and frozen pasta ("冷食のパスタしか勝たん♪", i0079); buys too much on a friend's
      recommendation (i0078); strawberry pyjamas (i0071); no Christmas presents "for years" (i0026); worries about exams and misses the
      econometrics final (i0076, i0133).
    - Speech: 私; "萌智子", "芽吹ちゃん", "やす子ちゃん"; mock-formal わたくし (i0020); 傍点 emphasis ("織・り・込・み済み", "軽・く・"); childlike
      fragments in shock ("あんな…", "だめ…", "見ちゃだめ…", "1億ってなに…"); です in crisis ("死んでないです!", i0136); "ノープロブレム!!";
      self-commands ("がんばれ私 逃げるな私", "よし…やろう…").
    - Cognition under differing conditions: an official promise → "さっすがスイス", "介入パワー" (i0057, i0071); a named risk → "織り込み済み
      のはず!" (i0072–i0073); the catastrophe → reflex avoidance and bodily collapse (i0106–i0121); the demand → "bug", litany, fear wall,
      six-digit hope (i0125–i0129), then "払えるわけない" (i0132); acceptance ("自己責任だもん", i0140) → symmetry → a 1億 target, the tax money,
      unlimited leverage, seven doublings (i0142–i0148); recognition of repetition ("お母さんとおんなじ", i0147) followed by resignation.
    - Directional ties: Mochiko (affection, admiration, trust without knowledge); Mebuki (warmth, admiration, food after her loss); Yasuko
      (teasing; answered her question only with "お金ない"); her mother (identification become repetition); her father (relief at living
      alone; concealment resolved: "誰にも迷惑かけちゃダメだ").

      "そこは止めろよ…! 萌智子…!", i0143; a pretext and a direct request to meet, i0147–i0149); Kurumi (receives and acts on her
      warning, i0076, i0130).

    - Money: 71,052 (i0047); no position; exit declared, not executed.
    - Past: the i0046 collage includes あふぃちゃん's V01 trades among her own memories (consistent with V02-C009, OBSERVATION HIGH since
      V02), unspoken among the characters; "そもそも本当に前からFXやっていたし" (i0136).

    - Cognition: self-blame without accusation ("自滅したマヌケ", i0014); deceived and grateful at once (i0018–i0019); enforces the loophole
      with glee as the contest's originator (i0025).
    - Directional ties: Mochiko (wished to win together; moved by the bare name; thanks unspoken); Kurumi and Yasuko (partial disclosure);
      her parents (opposed her leaving university, i0008).
    - Money: contest 613,200 → −3,400,000 (654.4%減) (i0012) = 0円 less 3,400,000 of post-base deposits (CALC on accepted V03–V04 boxes);
      debts and lost tuition (i0008); no repayment amount printed.
    - Reassessment threshold: disclosure of the liquidation and the lender; repayment; her knowledge of Kurumi's loss.
